Projected minimax algorithm:
Given z. Repeat:
- h=0.5(1+z); c_valid via FFT.
- active: all indices with c >= m - tol (tol e.g. 1e-4 or 0.001*m) or top K if many. Use weights uniform (or softmax with high alpha).
- grad = average derivative of active c's (hard max subgradient).
- For step: trial = project(z - eta*grad). Compute C. If improvement, accept; else reduce eta.
- Also maybe use smoothed weights with temperature (softmax) for better direction; but gradient of hard max average is okay.
- Since objective nonconvex, use small eta, many iterations. Need select eta. c scale ~0.3; grad scale? For h, derivative components sum magnitude O(1), normalized; eta in z maybe 0.05? Try line search over [eta*0.5^q].
- Since active set may be few, gradient has pattern: for shift s, grad like +1 in one segment, -1 in another. Moving z - eta*grad tends to increase h where c wants reduce? Need check sign. If c_s high, want reduce c_s. c_s=∑ h_i(1-h_{i+s}). For a positive shift, derivative wrt h_i positive in first segment (increasing h_i increases c), negative in second (increasing h_{i+s} decreases c). To reduce c, decrease h_i in first (grad positive => z - eta*grad decreases z, good) and increase h_i in second (grad negative => z - eta*grad increases z). This means shift mass from first to second for shift s. Good.

Use projected z to mean zero. Step size: Maybe start eta=0.1, squeeze. For nonconvex, can use backtracking: try eta, eta*0.7 until improve or min. If no improvement, reduce.

```
def project_z(z,n):
    z=np.clip(z,-1,1)
    # target 0
    # find shift c: x=clip(z+c,-1,1) sum=0
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    # ensure sum at lo <=0? at c=-2, most -1, sum negative; at c=2 sum positive.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       x=np.clip(z+mid,-1,1)
       s=x.sum()
       if s>0: hi=mid else lo=mid
    return np.clip(z+(lo+hi)/2,-1,1)
```

Projection h with sum target n/2:
```
def project_h(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # bisection shift c to sum target
  target=n/2
  lo=-2; hi=2
  for _ in range(50):
    mid=(lo+hi)/2
    x=np.clip(h+mid,0,1)
    if x.sum()>target: hi=mid else lo=mid
  x=np.clip(h+(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
  diff=target-x.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-15:
     interior=np.where((x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12))[0]
     if len(interior):
        # choose index with enough room in direction
        if diff>0:
           idx=interior[np.argmax(1-x[interior])]
           add=min(diff,1-x[idx]); x[idx]+=add
        else:
           idx=interior[np.argmin(x[interior])]
           sub=min(-diff,x[idx]); x[idx]-=sub
  return x
```
Bisection 50 vector ops. Could speed with sorting? Maybe okay. But in line search 6 projections per iter, 5000 iters =>300k vector ops length 4096 ->1.2B element ops (NumPy C) okay maybe a few seconds? Actually 300k * 4096 =1.2e9, NumPy ~10 GB/s ->0.1s? Python overhead 300k calls ~ maybe 5-10s. Fine.

Can reduce bisection to 35 iterations for 1e-11 sum precision (range 4, 35 bits). Use 40.

`run_minimax_gradient(h,n,deadline, nmax_iter=?)`:
```
h=project_h; best=corr_max_fft; zerr? 
step=0.5; no_imp=0; rng
for it in range(max_iter):
  if time> deadline: break
  # compute c_valid & max
  c_full=corr_full(h)
  c_valid = np.concatenate((c[:n], c[-n+1:]))
  m=c_valid.max()
  # active
  tol = max(1e-8, 1e-4*m)
  idx=np.where(c_valid >= m - tol)[0]
  if len(idx)>40: idx=idx[np.argpartition(-c_valid[idx],40)][:40]
  # weights
  temp = np.array([1e-4,1e-3,0.005,0.05]? choose by cycle)
  # weights = exp((c_valid[idx]-m)/temp); normalize
  grad = zeros(n)
  for w,k in zip(weights, valid_k[idx]):
     s=...
     scale=w
     if s>=0...
  grad -= grad.mean()
  gnorm=...
  if gnorm==0 break
  d=grad/gnorm
  # line search steps: maybe [step, step*0.6, ...]
  accepted=False
  for mult in [1,0.6,0.36,0.216,0.129,0.077]:
     trial=project_h(h - mult*step*d, n)
     nc=corr_max_fft(trial,n)
     if nc < best - 1e-15:
        best=nc; h=trial; accepted=True; break
  if accepted:
     no_imp=0
     step=min(step*1.1,2.0)
  else:
     no_imp+=1
     step*=0.7
     if no_imp>20:
        # perturb
        for noise_level in [0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005]:
           noise = rng.normal(0,noise_level,n)
           # lowpass? could just random; project kills mean
           trial=project_h(h+noise)
           nc=corr_max_fft(trial)
           if nc<best: best=nc; h=trial; break
        else:
           # If still no, try a slightly coarse decimate/upsample? 
        if no_imp>60: maybe break to next candidate
  if m <= target? we can stop early? We need lower than current record; if best < 0.3808 maybe can stop and return. But keep small time for exact.
```

Let's design code skeleton:
```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

_CACHE = {} maybe not needed

def compute_c5_exact(h,n):
    if n==1: return float(h[0]*(1-h[0])*2)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h,1-h,mode='full')*(2.0/n)))

def c5_fft_full(h,n):
    N = 1 << (2*n - 1).bit_length()
    Fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    Fg=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fh*np.conj(Fg), N)
    return c

def c5_fft_max(h,n,N):
    Fh=...
    c=...
    m1=c[:n].max()
    m2=c[-(n-1):].max()
    return float(max(m1,m2))
```

```
def precompute_fft_data(n):
  N=1 << (2*n-1).bit_length()
  valid_k=np.concatenate((np.arange(n), np.arange(N-n+1,N)))
  return N, valid_k
```

Active derivative function:
```
def active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,valid_k,N):
    grad=np.zeros(n,float)
    for wi,k in zip(weights, valid_k):
       if wi<=0: continue
       if k < n:
          s=-int(k)
          if s==0: ...
       else:
          s=N-int(k)
       if s>=0:
          t=s
          if t==0:
             grad += wi*(1.0-2.0*h) # note weight
          else:
             nv=n-t
             grad[:nv] += wi*(1.0 - h[t:])
             grad[t:] += -wi*h[:nv]
       else:
          t=-s
          nv=n-t
          grad[t:] += wi*(1.0 - h[:nv])
          grad[:nv] += -wi*h[t:]
    return grad
```
Weights sum 1 for active average. For s=0, formula above: (1-h)-h. Good.

For gradient of soft max over active with α: weights normalized; gradient = (1/α)*∑ weights ∂c. But if we use active average, no 1/α. For line search step L2 normalized, scale irrelevant. For L-BFGS Fourier, need gradient magnitude appropriate; can include factor maybe. For Fourier, if active objective is weighted average, gradient as active average. If we want soft max, include 1/α; but L-BFGS step scales magically. Use weighted average.

Let's implement `minimax_projected(h,n,N,valid_k,deadline, seed, step_max, target_best)` with parameters.
Pseudo:
```
def minimax_projected(h,n,N,valid_k,deadline, seed, max_time=None, tol_factor=1e-4, max_active=40, step0=0.6, stop_c=None):
    h = project_h(h,n)
    best = c5_fft_max(h,n,N,valid_k)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    step = step0
    no_imp = 0
    it=0
    t_start=time.time()
    while time.time()<deadline:
       if stop_c is not None and best <= stop_c: break
       cv = c_valid_fft(h,n,N,valid_k)
       m = float(cv.max())
       # if max active count
       tol = max(1e-9, tol_factor*m)
       idx = np.where(cv >= m - tol)[0]
       if idx.size > max_active:
          # pick top max_active
          part = np.argpartition(-cv[idx], max_active)
          idx = idx[part[:max_active]]
       # choose temp factor based on iteration/no_imp
       cycle = (it//5)%4
       temp = [0.0, 0.0005*m, 0.002*m, 0.008*m][cycle] maybe
       if temp <= 1e-12: weights = np.ones(idx.size)/idx.size
       else:
          w = np.exp((cv[idx]-m)/temp); weights=w/w.sum()
       grad = active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,valid_k[idx],N)
       grad -= grad.mean()
       gn = np.linalg.norm(grad)
       if gn < 1e-14: break
       d=grad/gn
       improved=False
       # Try several multipliers
       for mult in (1.0, 0.6, 0.36, 0.216, 0.129, 0.077, 0.046):
           trial = project_h(h - step*mult*d,n)
           nc = c5_fft_max(trial,n,N,valid_k)
           if nc < best - 1e-15:
              best=nc; h=trial; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          no_imp=0
          step = min(step*1.05, 1.5)
       else:
          no_imp +=1
          step *= 0.7
          # if many failed, try random active weights/noise
          if no_imp >= 10 and no_imp % 5 ==0:
             # random direction at top active? generate random weights in simplex
             for _ in range(8):
                randw = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(idx.size))
                grad2=active_gradient_h(h,n,randw,valid_k[idx],N)
                grad2-=grad2.mean(); gn2=...
                if gn2>0:
                  d2=grad2/gn2
                  for mult in [step, step*0.5, step*0.25]:
                     trial=project_h(h - step*mult*d2,n)
                     nc=...
                     if nc<best: update improved...
          if no_imp >= 30:
             # add random lowpass noise
             for amp in (0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002):
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, amp, n)
                # optional smooth noise by cumulative? Use random phase low frequencies
                # We can generate smooth noise via Fourier: white in Fourier first 20 modes
                # helper add_smooth_noise
                trial=project_h(h+noise,n)
                nc=...
                if nc<best: ...
             no_imp=0 # reset? or if still, break?
       it+=1
    return h,best
```
Use smooth random noise better: white noise may increase high-frequency and raise max. Generate low-frequency noise:

```
def smooth_noise(n, rng, max_mode=8):
   x=np.fft.rfftfreq(n)*2*np.pi? Actually modes.
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,max_mode+1):
      amp=rng.normal(0, 1.0/sqrt(m))
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      noise[ x? ]...
```
But simple white after projection maybe potential but not likely improve. Use lowpass: `noise = rng.normal(0,amp,n); for _ in range(3): noise = np.convolve(noise, [0.25,0.5,0.25], mode='same')` then center. But conv per few times okay.

```
def fourier_active_obj(theta, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu):
   y=theta @ B
   h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(y)
   cv=c_valid_fft(h,n,N) # returns full valid; but need active values
   val=float(weights @ cv[active_k]) + mu*(h.sum()-n/2.0)**2
   return val

def fourier_active_grad(theta, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu):
   y=...
   h=...
   sech2=1/np.cosh(y)**2 # use 1 - tanh^2 faster
   tanh_y=np.tanh(y); sech2=1-tanh_y*tanh_y
   dh=0.5*sech2
   gh=active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,active_k,N)  # weights sum 1
   q=gh*dh
   grad=B.dot(q)
   err=h.sum()-n/2.0
   if abs(err)>0:
      sumbase = B.dot(dh)
      grad += 2*mu*err*sumbase
   return grad
```

Let's design exact sum function:
```
def enforce_sum_exact(x,n,target):
    # x in [0,1]
    x = x.copy()
    for _ in range(3):
        s=x.sum()
        diff=target-s
        if abs(diff) <= 1e-12: break
        interior = np.where((x>1e-8)&(x<1-1e-8))[0]
        if interior.size==0:
           # if all at bounds but diff, cannot; choose one bound and nudge
           if diff>0: idx=int(np.argmin(x)); x[idx]=min(1.0,x[idx]+diff)
           else: idx=int(np.argmax(x)); x[idx]=max(0.0,x[idx]-diff)
        else:
           # distribute among up to 32 interior with largest room
           if diff>0:
              rooms=1-x[interior]
              order=interior[np.argsort(-rooms)[:32]]
              add=diff/len(order)
              for idx in order:
                 room=1-x[idx]
                 inc=min(room,add)
                 x[idx]+=inc
                 diff-=inc
                 if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
           else:
              order=interior[np.argsort(x[interior])[:32]]
              sub=-diff/len(order)
              for idx in order:
                 dec=min(x[idx],sub)
                 x[idx]-=dec
                 diff+=dec
                 if abs(diff)<1e-15: break
    return np.clip(x,0,1)
```
Use in project after bisection.

For project with bisection, target for h. Could use z target 0? h target maybe non-integer if n odd; use target=n/2. Good.

```
def make_default_h(n,seed):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
  h=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,7):
     amp = 0.4/range?
     amp = rng.uniform(0.2,0.6)/m? maybe
     phase = rng.uniform(0,2π)
     sign = rng.choice([-1,1])
     h += amp * np.cos(np.pi*m*x+phase)
  h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(2*h)
  return project_h(h,n)
```

```
def c_valid_fft(h,n,N,valid_k):
    Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    Fg = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.conj(Fg), N)
    return c[valid_k]
```
`c5_fft_max` can call this and max; but for line search, avoid valid_k indexing? Use:
```
def c5_fft_max(h,n,N):
  Fh=...; Fg=...; c=irfft; return max(c[:n].max(), c[-(n-1):].max())
```

Random lowpass noise function:
```
def make_lowpass_noise(n, rng, base_amp, max_mode=6):
    noise = np.zeros(n)
    modes = np.arange(1, max_mode+1)
    amps = rng.normal(0,1,size=len(modes)) / np.sqrt(modes)
    phases = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi,size=len(modes))
    idx = np.arange(n)
    # Use x grid? For each mode, add amp*cos(2π*m*idx/n + phase)? Since endpoints periodic. But natural frequencies m? Use 2π*m*idx/n to be periodic over n. This corresponds to shift frequencies maybe not πm*x but any.
    for m,a,p in zip(modes,amps,phases): noise += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*idx/n+p)
    return base_amp*noise/np.linalg.norm(noise or 1)
```

Let's draft code mentally.

```
def project_h(h,n):
    x=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    target=n/2.0
    s=x.sum()
    if abs(s-target)<1e-12:
       return enforce_sum_exact(x,n,target)
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    # ensure function monotonic; at lo clip(h-2) maybe 0 all sum<=target, at hi sum>=target
    # But if h all 0 and s=0 target n/2, at hi=2 all 1 sum n >target.
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       y=np.clip(x+mid,0.0,1.0)
       sy=y.sum()
       if sy > target:
          hi=mid
       else:
          lo=mid
    x=np.clip(x+(lo+hi)*0.5,0.0,1.0)
    return enforce_sum_exact(x,n,target)
```
Bisection logic: If sy > target, we need smaller shift -> hi=mid; else lo=mid. Good. If initial s already near, skip? refine maybe. But if s slightly off, bisection still starts -2/2; okay. Could initialize lo/hi around s diff? but fine.

`enforce_sum_exact` loops 3. If x all at bounds and diff remains, small. Use `np.clip` final.

`active_gradient_h` implementation details:
```
def active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,ks,N):
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    for wi,k in zip(weights,ks):
        if wi <= 1e-15: continue
        k=int(k)
        if k < n:
            s=-k
        else:
            s=N-k
        if s == 0:
            grad += wi*(1.0-2.0*h)
        elif s > 0:
            nv=n-s
            if nv <=0: continue
            grad[:nv] += wi*(1.0 - h[s:])
            grad[s:] += -wi*h[:nv]
        else:
            t=-s
            nv=n-t
            if nv <=0: continue
            grad[t:] += wi*(1.0 - h[:nv])
            grad[:nv] += -wi*h[t:]
    return grad
```
For s positive near n-1, nv=1, slices okay. For s negative, t≤n-1. Good.

`minimax_projected`:
```
def minimax_projected(h,n,N,valid_k,deadline,seed,stop_c=None):
    h=project_h(h,n)
    best=c5_fft_max(h,n,N)
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    step=0.8
    no_imp=0
    it=0
    last_noise=it
    while time.time() < deadline:
       if stop_c is not None and best <= stop_c: break
       cv=c_valid_fft(h,n,N,valid_k)
       m=float(cv.max())
       # if m maybe <= stop
       tol=max(1e-9, 1e-5*m)
       idx=np.where(cv >= m - tol)[0]
       if idx.size == 0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(cv))])
       # mode choose
       mode=it % 5
       if mode == 0:
          if idx.size>40: part=np.argpartition(-cv[idx],40); idx=idx[part[:40]]
          temp=0.002*m
       elif mode == 1:
          # top 1
          top=np.argmax(cv); idx=np.array([top])
          temp=0.0
       elif mode == 2:
          if idx.size>12: part=np.argpartition(-cv[idx],12); idx=idx[part[:12]]
          temp=0.0005*m
       elif mode ==3:
          if idx.size>25: part=np.argpartition(-cv[idx],25); idx=idx[part[:25]]
          temp=0.008*m
       else:
          # random subset of top idx
          if idx.size>8:
             choose=rng.choice(idx.size,8,replace=False)
             idx=idx[choose]
          temp=0.002*m
       vals=cv[idx]
       if temp <= 1e-12 or idx.size <=1:
          weights=np.ones(idx.size,dtype=float); weights/=idx.size
       else:
          w=np.exp((vals-m)/temp)
          sumw=w.sum()
          if sumw <=0: weights=...
          else: weights=w/sumw
       grad=active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,valid_k[idx],N)
       grad -= grad.mean()
       gn=np.linalg.norm(grad)
       accepted_this_iter=False
       if gn > 1e-14:
          d=grad/gn
          for mult in (1.0,0.55,0.30,0.16,0.085,0.045):
             if time.time() > deadline: break
             trial=project_h(h - step*mult*d,n)
             nc=c5_fft_max(trial,n,N)
             if nc < best - 1e-15:
                best=nc; h=trial; accepted_this_iter=True; break
       if accepted_this_iter:
          no_imp=0
          step=min(step*1.08,2.0)
       else:
          no_imp += 1
          step *= 0.75
          if step < 0.02: step=0.02
          # random perturbation if no improve often
          if no_imp >= 8 and no_imp - last_noise >= 6:
             last_noise=no_imp
             improved_noise=False
             for amp in (0.12,0.06,0.03,0.015,0.007,0.003):
                noise=lowpass_noise(n,rng,amp,max_mode=6)
                for sgn in (1.0,-1.0):
                    if time.time() > deadline: break
                    trial=project_h(h + sgn*noise,n)
                    nc=c5_fft_max(trial,n,N)
                    if nc < best - 1e-15:
                       best=nc; h=trial; improved_noise=True; break
                if improved_noise: break
             if improved_noise:
                no_imp=0
             if no_imp >= 40:
                # try a larger moderate random and reset; if still no, maybe decrease stop? E.g. random reproject
                trial=project_h(h + lowpass_noise(n,rng,0.2,8),n)
                nc=...
                if nc < best: ...
                if no_imp > 80:
                    break
       it +=1
    h=project_h(h,n)
    return h,best
```
Need avoid `choice` on `idx.size` if smaller. Good.

This may be slow due `time.time()` calls; okay.

`lowpass_noise`:
```
def lowpass_noise(n,rng,amp,max_mode=6):
    idx=np.arange(n)
    noise=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,max_mode+1):
       a=rng.normal(0.0,1.0)
       p=rng.uniform(0.0,2.0*np.pi)
       noise += a*np.cos((2.0*np.pi*m*idx)/n + p)
    norm=np.linalg.norm(noise)
    if norm < 1e-12: return noise
    return (amp/noirm)*noise
```
No lambdas. Good. Could use sine too? Cos random enough.

`make_default_h`:

```
def make_default_h(n,seed):
  rng=...
  idx=np.arange(n)
  y=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1,11):
     amp=rng.uniform(0.1,0.6)/max(1.0,m**0.5)
     ph=rng.uniform(0,2π)
     y += amp*np.cos(2π*m*idx/n+ph)
  h=0.5+0.5*np.tanh(1.5*y)
  return project_h(h,n)
```

Gradient:
```
def fourier_active_grad(theta,B,n,N,active_k,weights,mu):
   y=B.dot(theta); tanh_y=np.tanh(y); h=0.5+0.5*tanh_y; sech2=(1.0-tanh_y*tanh_y)
   gh=active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,active_k,N) # active_k are full indices, weights sum 1
   dh=0.5*sech2
   q=gh*dh
   grad=B.dot(q)
   err=h.sum()-n/2.0
   if err != 0:
       sumbase=B.dot(dh)
       grad += 2.0*mu*err*sumbase
   return grad
```
However objective uses weighted average of c at active_k. `active_gradient_h` expects `ks` full and weights correspond. Good.

`upsample_h` robust:
```
def resample_h(h,nout):
    n=len(h)
    if n==nout: return h.copy()
    x_out=np.linspace(0,2,nout,endpoint=False)
    x_in=np.linspace(0,2,n,endpoint=False)
    hr=np.interp(x_out,x_in,h)
    return project_h(hr,nout)
```
Use for up/down. If n=1? fine.

Let's outline run time with internal deadlines:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
 t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+budget_s-70
 n_main=2048
 N_m,valid_m=precompute_fft(n_main)
 # initial
 ...
 h_main = resample(h0,n_main)
 c_main = c5_fft_max(...)
 best_main_h=h_main; best_main_c_internal=c_main
 # maybe if initial exact lower, keep best_init_exact.
 # Stage 1 direct gradient
 d1 = t0 + 420
 h1,c1 = minimax_projected(h_main,n_main,N_m,valid_m,d1,seed)
 if c1<best_main_c_internal: update
 # Stage 2 Fourier at n_four=1024 if time
 if time.time() < deadline - 200:
   Nf,validf=precompute_fft(1024)
   hf_start = resample(h_main,1024) # or best from main
   # Maybe also run from initial? h_main.
   d2 = min(time.time()+180, deadline-300) if time.time()+180 < deadline-300 else ... 
   hf,cf = optimize_fourier(hf_start,1024,Nf,validf,d2,seed+1)
   h_main_up = resample(hf,n_main)
   # short gradient on h_main_up
   d2b = min(time.time()+80, deadline-280)
   h2b,c2b = minimax_projected(h_main_up,n_main,N_m,valid_m,d2b,seed+2)
   if c2b < best_main_c_internal: update
 # Stage 3 final high res 4096 if time
 if time.time() < deadline - 150:
   n_final=4096; Nf2,validf2=precompute_fft(n_final)
   h_fin = resample(best_main_h, n_final)
   d3 = min(time.time()+250, deadline-80)
   h_fin,cf_final = minimax_projected(h_fin,n_final,Nf2,validf2,d3,seed+3, stop_c=0.3807)
   # if cf_final internal good keep high_res candidate
 # Final exact evaluate main and high_res and initial; choose min exact
 candidates = [(best_main_h,n_main)] + maybe [(h_fin,n_final)] + [(h_init,n_init)]
 best_exact=inf
 for h,n in candidates:
    h=project_h(h,n)
    ce=compute_c5_exact(h,n)
    if ce<best_exact: ...
 return best_h,best_exact,best_n
```

Softmax weights:
```
def softmax_weights(vals,m,temp):
  if temp <= 1e-12 or vals.size==1: return ones/size
  sh=vals-m
  mx=sh.max()
  w=np.exp(sh-mx)
  s=w.sum()
  if s<=0: return ones/size
  return w/s
```

Pseudo run in detail:
```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    hard = t0 + budget_s - 2
    opt_deadline = t0 + budget_s - 35
    have_init=False
    h_init=None; n_init=0; c_init_exact=None
    try:
       h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       n_init=len(h_init)
       if n_init>0: have_init=True
    except Exception:
       pass
    if have_init:
       h_init=project_h(h_init,n_init)
       c_init_exact=compute_c5_exact(h_init,n_init)
    # choose n_main based on budget
    n_main = 2048
    N_m, valid_m = precompute_fft(n_main)
    if have_init:
       h_main = resample_h(h_init,n_main)
       c_main = c5_fft_max(h_main,n_main,N_m)
    else:
       h_main = make_default_h(n_main,seed)
       c_main = c5_fft_max(...)
    best_main_h=h_main.copy(); best_main_c= c_main
    # Stage 0 if no init, maybe quick Fourier? skip.
    work = max(50, budget_s - 60)
    # Stage1
    if have_init and budget_s > 100:
        end1 = t0 + 0.38*work
    else:
        end1 = t0 + 0.50*work
    if end1 > opt_deadline: end1=opt_deadline
    h1,c1 = minimax_projected(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,valid_m,end1,seed,stop_c=0.3807)
    if c1 < best_main_c - 1e-14: update
    # Stage2 Fourier
    rem = opt_deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 150 and best_main_c > 0.3807:
       n_four=1024
       N_f,valid_f=precompute_fft(n_four)
       hf = resample_h(best_main_h,n_four)
       four_time = min(rem*0.55, 0.25*work)
       if four_time > 60:
          end2 = time.time()+four_time
          if end2 > opt_deadline-100: end2=opt_deadline-100
          hf2,cf2 = optimize_fourier(hf,n_four,N_f,valid_f,end2,seed+101,stop_c=0.3807)
          h_up = resample_h(hf2,n_main)
          short_time = min(opt_deadline-time.time()-80, 0.10*work)
          if short_time>30:
             h_up2,c_up2 = minimax_projected(h_up,n_main,N_m,valid_m,time.time()+short_time,seed+202,stop_c=0.3807)
             if c_up2 < best_main_c - 1e-14: update
          elif cf2 internal? Need if h_up not improved but hf c maybe? Compare c5_fft_max(h_up).
    # Stage3 final high-res
    n_final=4096
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 120:
       N_fin,valid_fin=precompute_fft(n_final)
       h_fin = resample_h(best_main_h,n_final)
       fin_time = min(rem*0.8, 0.28*work)
       if fin_time > 30:
          end3 = time.time()+fin_time
          if end3>opt_deadline: end3=opt_deadline
          h_fin,c_fin = minimax_projected(h_fin,n_final,N_fin,valid_fin,end3,seed+303,stop_c=0.3807)
          store high candidate
    # finalize
    candidates=[]
    if have_init: candidates.append((h_init,n_init))
    candidates.append((best_main_h,n_main))
    if high candidate: candidates.append((h_fin,n_final))
    best_h=None; best_exact=2.0
    for h,n in candidates:
       h=project_h(h,n)
       ce=compute_c5_exact(h,n)
       if ce < best_exact - 1e-15:
          best_exact=ce; best_h=h.copy(); best_n=n
    if best_h is None: ...
    return best_h,best_exact,int(best_n)
```

`make_binary_start`:
```
def make_binary_start(h,n):
   hb=np.zeros(n)
   k=n//2
   if k>0:
      idx=np.argsort(-h)
      hb[idx[:k]]=1.0
      rem=n/2.0 - hb.sum()
      if abs(rem)>1e-12 and k<n:
          hb[idx[k]] += rem
   return project_h(hb,n)
```
For n even, target n/2 integer, k=n/2, rem 0. Good. If h ties, arbitrary.
Run minimax on binary maybe uses same? Gradient on binary may move fractional. Good.

Detailed code:

```
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time

def compute_c5_exact(h,n):
    if n <= 0: return 1.0
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full') * (2.0/float(n))))
```
`np.max` of length if n=1 works. Good.

`project_h` as above. In `enforce_sum_exact`, we need n? target. Use loops.

```
def enforce_sum_exact(x,n,target):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    for _ in range(4):
       s=float(x.sum())
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
          break
       interior=np.where((x>1e-10)&(x<1.0-1e-10))[0]
       if interior.size == 0:
          if diff>0:
             idx=int(np.argmin(x))
             x[idx]=min(1.0,float(x[idx])+diff)
          else:
             idx=int(np.argmax(x))
             x[idx]=max(0.0,float(x[idx])+diff)
       else:
          count=min(64,int(interior.size))
          if diff>0:
             rooms=1.0-x[interior]
             order=interior[np.argsort(-rooms)[:count]]
          else:
             order=interior[np.argsort(x[interior])[:count]]
          step=diff/float(len(order))
          for idx in order:
             idx=int(idx)
             if diff>0:
                room=1.0-float(x[idx])
                inc=min(room,step)
                x[idx]+=inc
                diff-=inc
             else:
                dec=min(float(x[idx]),-step)
                x[idx]-=dec
                diff+=dec
             if abs(diff) < 1e-15: break
    np.clip(x,0.0,1.0,out=x)
    return x
```
Potential issue: When diff>0, using same `step=diff/len(order)`, after distributing some, diff changes but step remains initial; okay if not using list of increments individually? It just adds same to all up to room. If one hits bound, residual not redistributed within same pass? We break when diff small. If one bound, step too small? Could leave residual if one hit bound. Loop 4 redistributes. Good.

Bisection in project_h: Use `x0 = np.clip(x,0,1)`. But `x` already clipped. If sum diff, bisection. Could initialize lo, hi based on diff to reduce: lo=-1.0, hi=1.0 maybe enough because to change sum from 0 to n needs shift 1; target half and h in [0,1] so shift in [-1,1]. But use [-2,2] safe. 45 iter.

`precompute_fft(n)`:
```
def precompute_fft(n):
    N=1 << max(1,(2*n-1).bit_length())
    valid_k=np.concatenate((np.arange(n,dtype=np.int64), np.arange(N-n+1,N,dtype=np.int64)))
    ones=np.zeros(N,dtype=float); ones[:n]=1.0
    Fones=np.fft.rfft(ones,N)
    return N, valid_k, Fones
```
If 2n-1=1 for n=1, bit_length=1, N=2; `N-n+1=2`; last empty. okay.

`c_full_fft(h,N,Fones)`:
```
def c_full_fft(h,N,Fones):
    Fh=np.fft.rfft(h,N)
    Fg=Fones - Fh
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fh*np.conj(Fg),N)
    return c
```
Could ensure h is float and N power of two. Good.

```
idx=np.arange(n)
noise=np.zeros(n)
for m in range(1,max_mode+1):
    a1=rng.normal(0,1); p1=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    a2=rng.normal(0,1); p2=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
    noise += a1*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*idx/n + p1) + a2*np.sin(...)
norm=np.linalg.norm(noise)
if norm<1e-15: return noise
return (amp/norm)*noise
```

Fourier basis:
```
def prepare_basis(n,M):
    x = np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
    B = np.ones((2*M+1,n), dtype=float)
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       a=np.pi*m*x
       B[2*m-1]=np.cos(a)
       B[2*m]=np.sin(a)
    return B
```
`fit_theta`:
```
def fit_theta(h,B):
    z=np.clip(2.0*h-1.0, -0.9, 0.9)
    y=np.arctanh(z)
    theta=np.linalg.lstsq(B,y,rcond=None)[0]
    theta=np.clip(theta,-10.0,10.0)
    return theta
```

`fourier_active_obj` and grad. Need pass `active_k` as np array.
```
def fourier_active_obj(theta,B,N,active_k,weights,mu,Fones):
    y=B.dot(theta)
    hy=np.tanh(y)
    h=0.5+0.5*hy
    c=c_full_fft(h,N,Fones)
    return float(np.dot(weights,c[active_k]) + mu*(float(h.sum())-float(B.shape[1])/2.0)**2)
```
Use `n = B.shape[1]` to avoid param? But we need n for target; can pass n. Simpler pass n.

In grad:
```
def fourier_active_grad(theta,B,n,N,active_k,weights,mu,Fones):
    y=B.dot(theta)
    hy=np.tanh(y)
    h=0.5+0.5*hy
    sech2=1.0 - hy*hy
    dh=0.5*sech2
    gh=active_gradient_h(h,n,weights,active_k,N)
    q=gh*dh
    grad=B.dot(q)
    err=float(h.sum()) - n/2.0
    if abs(err) > 1e-14:
        sumbase=B.dot(dh)
        grad += 2.0*mu*err*sumbase
    return grad
```
Potential issue: If y large, hy^2=1, sech2=0; q zero. Good.

Now, `resample_h`: Use `np.interp` requires xp increasing. If n=1? x_in [0]. For upsample from 1 to n, `np.interp` works? It expects xp length; yes returns constant maybe. Use endpoint=False. For n=1, x_in=0, x_out 0..2-2/n? `np.interp` handles. Not important.
```
def resample_h(h,nout):
   n=len(h)
   if n == nout: return project_h(h,nout)
   if n == 0: return np.empty(nout)
   x_in=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n,endpoint=False)
   x_out=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,nout,endpoint=False)
   if n == 1:
       hr=np.full(nout,float(h[0]))
   else:
       hr=np.interp(x_out,x_in,h)
   return project_h(hr,nout)
```

Now, run function with dynamic deadlines. Need careful using `Fones` variables.

Let's code run in more detail:
```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    hard_deadline=t0+budget_s-3
    opt_deadline=t0+budget_s-45
    if opt_deadline < t0+30: opt_deadline=t0+max(5,budget_s-3)
    have_init=False; h_init=None; n_init=0; c_init_exact=None
    try:
       h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if h_init.size>0: have_init=True; n_init=len(h_init)
    except Exception:
       have_init=False
    if have_init:
       h_init=project_h(h_init,n_init)
       c_init_exact=compute_c5_exact(h_init,n_init)
    # n_main choose
    if budget_s > 700:
       n_main=2048
    else: n_main=1024
    N_m,valid_m,F_m=precompute_fft(n_main)
    if have_init:
       h_main=resample_h(h_init,n_main)
    else:
       h_main=make_default_h(n_main, seed)
       # choose better of two defaults maybe
       h_alt=make_default_h(n_main, seed+77)
       if c5_fft_max(h_alt,n_main,N_m,F_m) < c5_fft_max(h_main,n_main,N_m,F_m): h_main=h_alt
    c_main=c5_fft_max(h_main,n_main,N_m,F_m)
    best_main_h=h_main.copy(); best_main_c=c_main
    target_met=0.38075
    # Stage 1 direct
    if have_init:
       stage1_frac=0.34
    else:
       stage1_frac=0.25
    rem0=opt_deadline-t0
    end1=t0+stage1_frac*rem0
    if end1 > opt_deadline-20: end1=opt_deadline-20
    h1,c1=minimax_projected(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,end1,seed,stop_c=target_met)
    if c1 < best_main_c - 1e-14:
        best_main_c=c1; best_main_h=h1
    # Stage 2 binary
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 120 and best_main_c > target_met:
        h_bin=make_binary_start(best_main_h,n_main)
        cb=c5_fft_max(h_bin,n_main,N_m,F_m)
        # allocate 12% or up to rem-180? leave for Fourier/final
        bin_time=min(0.12*(opt_deadline-t0), rem-180 if rem-180>30 else max(30,rem*0.25))
        if bin_time>25:
           h2,c2=minimax_projected(h_bin,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,time.time()+bin_time,seed+11,stop_c=target_met)
           if c2 < best_main_c - 1e-14: best...
    # Stage 3 Fourier
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 150 and best_main_c > target_met:
       n_four = 1024 if n_main >= 2048 else n_main
       N_f,valid_f,F_f=precompute_fft(n_four)
       hf=resample_h(best_main_h,n_four)
       four_time=min(0.20*(opt_deadline-t0), rem-200 if rem-200>60 else max(60,rem*0.5))
       if four_time > 60:
          end2=time.time()+four_time
          if end2>opt_deadline-120: end2=opt_deadline-120
          hf2,cf2=optimize_fourier(hf,n_four,N_f,valid_f,F_f,end2,seed+21,M=10,K=14,mu=5.0,stop_c=target_met)
          h_up=resample_h(hf2,n_main)
          cu=c5_fft_max(h_up,n_main,N_m,F_m)
          if cu < best_main_c -1e-14: best...
          rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
          if rem > 90 and best_main_c > target_met:
             short_time=min(0.08*(opt_deadline-t0), rem-90)
             if short_time>25:
                h3,c3=minimax_projected(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,time.time()+short_time,seed+31,stop_c=target_met)
                if c3 < best_main_c: update
    # Stage 4 final highres
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 150 and best_main_c > target_met:
       if n_main == 2048:
          n_final=4096
       else:
          n_final=2048
       N_fin,valid_fin,F_fin=precompute_fft(n_final)
       h_fin=resample_h(best_main_h,n_final)
       fin_time=min(0.30*(opt_deadline-t0), rem-80)
       if fin_time > 60:
          end3=time.time()+fin_time
          if end3>opt_deadline: end3=opt_deadline
          h4,c4=minimax_projected(h_fin,n_final,N_fin,valid_fin,F_fin,end3,seed+41,stop_c=target_met)
          store high candidate
    # final exact
    candidates=[]
    if have_init: candidates.append((h_init,n_init))
    candidates.append((best_main_h,n_main))
    if high candidate set: candidates.append((h_fin,n_final))
    best_h=None; best_exact=2.0; best_n=0
    for h,n in candidates:
       if time.time() > hard_deadline-10:
           # just compute first? But need return. If no best, compute current maybe.
       h=project_h(h,n)
       ce=compute_c5_exact(h,n)
       if ce < best_exact:
           ...
    if best_h is None:
       best_h=project_h(best_main_h,n_main); best_exact=compute_c5_exact(best_h,n_main); best_n=n_main
    return best_h, float(best_exact), int(best_n)
```

```
def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    hard_deadline=t0+max(5.0,float(budget_s)-3.0)
    opt_deadline=t0+max(5.0,float(budget_s)-45.0)
    if opt_deadline > t0+max(1.0,float(budget_s)-5.0): opt_deadline=t0+max(1.0,float(budget_s)-5.0)
    have_init=False
    h_init=None; n_init=0; c_init_exact=None
    try:
       h_init=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if h_init.size>0:
          have_init=True; n_init=int(len(h_init))
    except Exception:
       pass
    if have_init:
       h_init=project_h(h_init,n_init)
       c_init_exact=compute_c5_exact(h_init,n_init)
    if float(budget_s) > 700.0:
       n_main=2048
    else:
       n_main=1024
    N_m,valid_m,F_m=precompute_fft(n_main)
    if have_init:
       h_main=resample_h(h_init,n_main)
    else:
       h_main=make_default_h(n_main, int(seed))
       c_h_main0=c5_fft_max(h_main,n_main,N_m,F_m)
       h_alt=make_default_h(n_main, int(seed)+77)
       if c5_fft_max(h_alt,n_main,N_m,F_m) < c_h_main0: h_main=h_alt
    c_main=c5_fft_max(h_main,n_main,N_m,F_m)
    best_main_h=h_main.copy(); best_main_c=float(c_main)
    target_met=0.38075
    work_total=max(1.0,opt_deadline-t0)
    # stage1
    frac1=0.34 if have_init else 0.22
    end1=t0+frac1*work_total
    if end1 > opt_deadline-15: end1=opt_deadline-15
    if end1 > t0:
       h1,c1=minimax_projected(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,end1,int(seed),stop_c=target_met)
       if float(c1) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
          best_main_c=float(c1); best_main_h=h1
    # binary
    high_h=None; high_n=0
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 300.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
       desired=0.10*work_total
       bin_time=min(desired, rem-250.0)
       if bin_time <= 30.0: bin_time=max(30.0, rem*0.15)
       if bin_time > 25.0:
          h_bin=make_binary_start(best_main_h,n_main)
          cbase=c5_fft_max(h_bin,n_main,N_m,F_m)
          h2,c2=minimax_projected(h_bin,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,time.time()+bin_time,int(seed)+11,stop_c=target_met)
          if float(c2) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
             best_main_c=float(c2); best_main_h=h2
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 180.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
       n_four = 1024 if n_main >= 2048 else n_main
       N_f,valid_f,F_f=precompute_fft(n_four)
       hf=resample_h(best_main_h,n_four)
       desired=0.20*work_total
       four_time=min(desired, rem-220.0)
       if four_time <= 60.0: four_time=max(60.0, rem*0.4)
       if four_time > 40.0:
          end2=time.time()+four_time
          if end2 > opt_deadline-110: end2=opt_deadline-110
          hf2,cf2=optimize_fourier(hf,n_four,N_f,valid_f,F_f,end2,int(seed)+21,M=10,K=14,mu=5.0,stop_c=target_met)
          h_up=resample_h(hf2,n_main)
          cu=c5_fft_max(h_up,n_main,N_m,F_m)
          if float(cu) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
             best_main_c=float(cu); best_main_h=h_up
          rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
          if rem > 90.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
             desired=0.08*work_total
             short_time=min(desired, rem-80.0)
             if short_time <= 25.0: short_time=max(25.0, rem*0.25)
             if short_time > 20.0:
                h3,c3=minimax_projected(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,valid_m,F_m,time.time()+short_time,int(seed)+31,stop_c=target_met)
                if float(c3) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
                   best_main_c=float(c3); best_main_h=h3
    rem=opt_deadline-time.time()
    if rem > 150.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
       if n_main >= 2048:
          n_final=4096
       else:
          n_final=2048
       N_fin,valid_fin,F_fin=precompute_fft(n_final)
       h_fin=resample_h(best_main_h,n_final)
       desired=0.30*work_total
       fin_time=min(desired, rem-70.0)
       if fin_time <= 50.0: fin_time=max(50.0, rem*0.5)
       if fin_time > 40.0:
          end3=time.time()+fin_time
          if end3 > opt_deadline: end3=opt_deadline
          h4,c4=minimax_projected(h_fin,n_final,N_fin,valid_fin,F_fin,end3,int(seed)+41,stop_c=target_met)
          if float(c4) < (c5_fft_max(best_main_h,n_main,N_m,F_m) if False else 1.0): # not needed
          high_h=h4; high_n=n_final
          # We could also if c4 lower than best_main_c? Different n; exact later. Store regardless. If c4 < best_main_c maybe set best_main? no exact later.
    # final exact
    best_h=project_h(best_main_h,n_main)
    best_exact=compute_c5_exact(best_h,n_main)
    best_n=n_main
    if have_init:
       if c_init_exact < best_exact:
          best_h=project_h(h_init,n_init); best_exact=c_init_exact; best_n=n_init
    if high_h is not None:
       if time.time() < hard_deadline-10:
          high_h=project_h(high_h,high_n)
          ce=compute_c5_exact(high_h,high_n)
          if ce < best_exact:
             best_h=high_h; best_exact=ce; best_n=high_n
    # ensure budget
    return best_h, float(best_exact), int(best_n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize
import time


def compute_c5_exact(h, n):
    if n <= 0:
        return 1.0
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return float(np.max(np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * (2.0 / float(n))))


def enforce_sum_exact(x, n, target):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)

    for _ in range(4):
        s = float(x.sum())
        diff = float(target) - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break

        interior = np.where((x > 1e-10) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-10))[0]
        if interior.size == 0:
            if diff > 0:
                idx = int(np.argmin(x))
                x[idx] = min(1.0, float(x[idx]) + diff)
            else:
                idx = int(np.argmax(x))
                x[idx] = max(0.0, float(x[idx]) + diff)
            continue

        count = min(64, int(interior.size))
        if diff > 0:
            rooms = 1.0 - x[interior]
            order = interior[np.argsort(-rooms)[:count]]
        else:
            order = interior[np.argsort(x[interior])[:count]]

        step = diff / float(len(order))
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if diff > 0:
                room = 1.0 - float(x[idx])
                inc = min(room, step)
                x[idx] += inc
                diff -= inc
            else:
                dec = min(float(x[idx]), -step)
                x[idx] -= dec
                diff += dec
            if abs(diff) < 1e-15:
                break

    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    return x


def project_h(h, n):
    x = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    target = n / 2.0

    s = float(x.sum())
    if abs(s - target) < 1e-10:
        return enforce_sum_exact(x, n, target)

    lo = -2.0
    hi = 2.0
    for _ in range(45):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        y = np.clip(x + mid, 0.0, 1.0)
        if float(y.sum()) > target:
            hi = mid
        else:
            lo = mid

    x = np.clip(x + 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)
    return enforce_sum_exact(x, n, target)


def precompute_fft(n):
    N = 1 << max(1, (2 * n - 1).bit_length())
    valid_k = np.concatenate((
        np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64),
        np.arange(N - n + 1, N, dtype=np.int64)
    ))
    ones = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
    ones[:n] = 1.0
    Fones = np.fft.rfft(ones, N)
    return N, valid_k, Fones


def c_full_fft(h, N, Fones):
    Fh = np.fft.rfft(h, N)
    Fg = Fones - Fh
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fh * np.conj(Fg), N)
    return c


def c_valid_fft(h, N, valid_k, Fones):
    c = c_full_fft(h, N, Fones)
    return c[valid_k]


def c5_fft_max(h, n, N, Fones):
    c = c_full_fft(h, N, Fones)
    m = float(c[:n].max())
    if n > 1:
        m2 = float(c[-(n - 1):].max())
        if m2 > m:
            m = m2
    return m


def top_local_indices(vals, K):
    size = int(vals.size)
    if K <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.int64)
    K = min(K, size)
    if size <= K:
        return np.arange(size, dtype=np.int64)
    part = np.argpartition(-np.asarray(vals), K - 1)
    return part[:K]


def softmax_weights(vals, m, temp):
    size = int(vals.size)
    if size == 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=float)
    if size == 1 or temp <= 1e-14:
        return np.ones(size, dtype=float) / size

    sh = np.asarray(vals, dtype=float) - float(m)
    mx = float(sh.max())
    w = np.exp(sh - mx)
    sw = float(w.sum())
    if sw <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(size, dtype=float) / size
    w /= sw
    return w


def active_gradient_h(h, n, weights, ks, N):
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    weights = np.asarray(weights, dtype=float)

    for wi, k in zip(weights, ks):
        if wi <= 1e-15:
            continue
        k = int(k)
        if k < n:
            s = -k
        else:
            s = N - k

        if s == 0:
            grad += wi * (1.0 - 2.0 * h)
        elif s > 0:
            nv = n - s
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            grad[:nv] += wi * (1.0 - h[s:])
            grad[s:] += -wi * h[:nv]
        else:
            t = -s
            nv = n - t
            if nv <= 0:
                continue
            grad[t:] += wi * (1.0 - h[:nv])
            grad[:nv] += -wi * h[t:]

    return grad


def lowpass_noise(n, rng, amp, max_mode=6):
    idx = np.arange(n)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, max_mode + 1):
        a1 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        p1 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        a2 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0)
        p2 = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        arg = (2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * idx) / float(n)
        noise += a1 * np.cos(arg + p1) + a2 * np.sin(arg + p2)

    norm = float(np.linalg.norm(noise))
    if norm < 1e-15:
        return noise
    return (float(amp) / norm) * noise


def minimax_projected(h, n, N, valid_k, Fones, deadline, seed, stop_c=None):
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best = float(c5_fft_max(h, n, N, Fones))
    if stop_c is not None and best <= float(stop_c):
        return h, best

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    step = 0.8
    no_imp = 0
    last_noise_iter = -999
    it = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if stop_c is not None and best <= float(stop_c):
            break

        cv = c_valid_fft(h, N, valid_k, Fones)
        m = float(cv.max())
        tol = max(1e-9, 1e-5 * m)
        broad = np.where(cv >= m - tol)[0]
        if broad.size == 0:
            broad = np.array([int(np.argmax(cv))], dtype=np.int64)

        mode = it % 5
        if mode == 0:
            idx = top_local_indices(cv[broad], 40)
            if broad.size > 1:
                idx = broad[idx]
            temp = 0.002 * m
        elif mode == 1:
            idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(cv))], dtype=np.int64)
            temp = 0.0
        elif mode == 2:
            idx = broad[top_local_indices(cv[broad], 12)]
            temp = 0.0005 * m
        elif mode == 3:
            idx = broad[top_local_indices(cv[broad], 25)]
            temp = 0.008 * m
        else:
            if broad.size > 8:
                sel = rng.choice(broad.size, 8, replace=False)
                idx = broad[sel]
            else:
                idx = broad
            temp = 0.002 * m

        vals = cv[idx]
        weights = softmax_weights(vals, m, temp)
        grad = active_gradient_h(h, n, weights, valid_k[idx], N)
        grad -= grad.mean()
        gn = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))

        accepted = False
        if gn > 1e-14:
            d = grad / gn
            for mult in (1.0, 0.55, 0.30, 0.16, 0.085, 0.045):
                if time.time() > deadline:
                    break
                trial = project_h(h - step * mult * d, n)
                nc = float(c5_fft_max(trial, n, N, Fones))
                if nc < best - 1e-12:
                    best = nc
                    h = trial
                    accepted = True
                    break

        if accepted:
            no_imp = 0
            step = min(step * 1.08, 2.0)
        else:
            no_imp += 1
            step = max(0.02, step * 0.75)

            if no_imp - last_noise_iter >= 8:
                last_noise_iter = no_imp
                improved_noise = False
                for amp in (0.12, 0.06, 0.03, 0.015, 0.007, 0.003):
                    noise = lowpass_noise(n, rng, amp, 6)
                    for sgn in (1.0, -1.0):
                        if time.time() > deadline:
                            break
                        trial = project_h(h + sgn * noise, n)
                        nc = float(c5_fft_max(trial, n, N, Fones))
                        if nc < best - 1e-12:
                            best = nc
                            h = trial
                            improved_noise = True
                            break
                    if improved_noise:
                        break

                if improved_noise:
                    no_imp = 0

            if no_imp > 120:
                break

        it += 1

    h = project_h(h, n)
    return h, best


def make_binary_start(h, n):
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    k = n // 2
    if k == 0:
        return project_h(hb, n)

    order = np.argsort(-h)
    hb[order[:k]] = 1.0
    remaining = n / 2.0 - float(hb.sum())
    if abs(remaining) > 1e-12 and k < n:
        hb[order[k]] += remaining
    return project_h(hb, n)


def make_default_h(n, seed):
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    idx = np.arange(n)
    y = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, 11):
        amp = rng.uniform(0.15, 0.65) / np.sqrt(float(m))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
        y += amp * np.cos((2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * idx) / float(n) + ph)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(1.5 * y)
    return project_h(h, n)


def resample_h(h, nout):
    n = int(len(h))
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(nout)
    if n == int(nout):
        return project_h(h, nout)

    if n == 1:
        hr = np.full(int(nout), float(h[0]), dtype=float)
    else:
        x_in = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
        x_out = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, int(nout), endpoint=False)
        hr = np.interp(x_out, x_in, h)
    return project_h(hr, int(nout))


def prepare_basis(n, M):
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=False)
    B = np.ones((2 * M + 1, n), dtype=float)
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        a = np.pi * float(m) * x
        B[2 * m - 1] = np.cos(a)
        B[2 * m] = np.sin(a)
    return B


def fit_theta(h, B):
    z = np.clip(2.0 * h - 1.0, -0.9, 0.9)
    y = np.arctanh(z)
    theta = np.linalg.lstsq(B, y, rcond=None)[0]
    return np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)


def fourier_active_obj(theta, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu, Fones):
    y = B.dot(theta)
    hy = np.tanh(y)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * hy
    c = c_full_fft(h, N, Fones)
    err = float(h.sum()) - n / 2.0
    return float(np.dot(weights, c[active_k]) + float(mu) * err * err)


def fourier_active_grad(theta, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu, Fones):
    y = B.dot(theta)
    hy = np.tanh(y)
    h = 0.5 + 0.5 * hy
    sech2 = 1.0 - hy * hy
    dh = 0.5 * sech2

    gh = active_gradient_h(h, n, weights, active_k, N)
    q = gh * dh
    grad = B.dot(q)

    err = float(h.sum()) - n / 2.0
    if abs(err) > 1e-14:
        sumbase = B.dot(dh)
        grad += 2.0 * float(mu) * err * sumbase
    return grad


def optimize_fourier(h, n, N, valid_k, Fones, deadline, seed, M=10, K=14, mu=5.0, stop_c=None):
    B = prepare_basis(n, M)
    h = project_h(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c5_fft_max(h, n, N, Fones))
    if stop_c is not None and best_c <= float(stop_c):
        return best_h, best_c

    theta = fit_theta(h, B)
    if float(np.linalg.norm(theta)) < 1e-3:
        rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 0.4, len(theta))
        theta[0] = 0.0
        theta = np.clip(theta, -10.0, 10.0)

    no_improve = 0
    round_idx = 0

    while time.time() < deadline:
        if stop_c is not None and best_c <= float(stop_c):
            break

        raw = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(B.dot(theta))
        hcur = project_h(raw, n)
        ccur = float(c5_fft_max(hcur, n, N, Fones))

        cv = c_valid_fft(hcur, N, valid_k, Fones)
        m = float(cv.max())
        idx = top_local_indices(cv, K)
        if idx.size == 0:
            break

        vals = cv[idx]
        temp = 0.001 * max(1e-6, m)
        weights = softmax_weights(vals, m, temp)
        active_k = valid_k[idx]

        rem = max(1.0, deadline - time.time())
        maxit = 80 if rem < 120.0 else 150
        bounds = [(-10.0, 10.0)] * int(len(theta))

        try:
            obj_old = fourier_active_obj(theta, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu, Fones)
            res = minimize(
                fourier_active_obj,
                theta,
                jac=fourier_active_grad,
                args=(B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu, Fones),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                bounds=bounds,
                options={"maxiter": maxit, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-12, "maxls": 24}
            )
            cand = res.x
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(cand)):
                cand = theta
        except Exception:
            cand = theta
            obj_new = None

        if np.all(np.isfinite(cand)) and not np.array_equal(cand, theta):
            hnew_raw = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.tanh(B.dot(cand))
            hnew = project_h(hnew_raw, n)
            cnew = float(c5_fft_max(hnew, n, N, Fones))

            if cnew < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_c = cnew
                best_h = hnew
                theta = cand
                no_improve = 0
            else:
                obj_new = fourier_active_obj(cand, B, n, N, active_k, weights, mu, Fones)
                if obj_new is not None and obj_new < obj_old - 1e-12:
                    theta = cand
                no_improve += 1
                if no_improve == 4:
                    theta = theta * 0.85
                    no_improve = 0

        if no_improve > 16:
            break
        round_idx += 1

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget_s = float(budget_s)
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s - 3.0)
    opt_deadline = t0 + max(5.0, budget_s - 45.0)
    if opt_deadline > t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0):
        opt_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, budget_s - 5.0)

    have_init = False
    h_init = None
    n_init = 0
    c_init_exact = None

    try:
        h_init = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h_init.ndim == 1 and h_init.size > 0:
            have_init = True
            n_init = int(h_init.size)
    except Exception:
        have_init = False

    if have_init:
        h_init = project_h(h_init, n_init)
        c_init_exact = float(compute_c5_exact(h_init, n_init))

    if budget_s > 700.0:
        n_main = 2048
    else:
        n_main = 1024

    N_m, valid_m, F_m = precompute_fft(n_main)

    if have_init:
        h_main = resample_h(h_init, n_main)
    else:
        h_main = make_default_h(n_main, int(seed))
        c_main0 = float(c5_fft_max(h_main, n_main, N_m, F_m))
        h_alt = make_default_h(n_main, int(seed) + 77)
        if float(c5_fft_max(h_alt, n_main, N_m, F_m)) < c_main0:
            h_main = h_alt

    c_main = float(c5_fft_max(h_main, n_main, N_m, F_m))
    best_main_h = h_main.copy()
    best_main_c = c_main
    high_h = None
    high_n = 0

    target_met = 0.38075
    work_total = max(1.0, opt_deadline - t0)

    frac1 = 0.34 if have_init else 0.22
    end1 = t0 + frac1 * work_total
    if end1 > opt_deadline - 15.0:
        end1 = opt_deadline - 15.0
    if end1 > t0:
        h1, c1 = minimax_projected(best_main_h, n_main, N_m, valid_m, F_m,
                                   end1, int(seed), stop_c=target_met)
        if float(c1) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
            best_main_c = float(c1)
            best_main_h = h1

    rem = opt_deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 300.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
        desired = 0.10 * work_total
        bin_time = min(desired, rem - 250.0)
        if bin_time <= 30.0:
            bin_time = max(30.0, rem * 0.15)
        if bin_time > 25.0:
            h_bin = make_binary_start(best_main_h, n_main)
            h2, c2 = minimax_projected(h_bin, n_main, N_m, valid_m, F_m,
                                       time.time() + bin_time, int(seed) + 11,
                                       stop_c=target_met)
            if float(c2) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
                best_main_c = float(c2)
                best_main_h = h2

    rem = opt_deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 180.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
        n_four = 1024 if n_main >= 2048 else n_main
        N_f, valid_f, F_f = precompute_fft(n_four)
        hf = resample_h(best_main_h, n_four)

        desired = 0.20 * work_total
        four_time = min(desired, rem - 220.0)
        if four_time <= 60.0:
            four_time = max(60.0, rem * 0.4)

        if four_time > 40.0:
            end2 = time.time() + four_time
            if end2 > opt_deadline - 110.0:
                end2 = opt_deadline - 110.0

            hf2, cf2 = optimize_fourier(hf, n_four, N_f, valid_f, F_f,
                                        end2, int(seed) + 21,
                                        M=10, K=14, mu=5.0, stop_c=target_met)
            h_up = resample_h(hf2, n_main)
            cu = float(c5_fft_max(h_up, n_main, N_m, F_m))
            if cu < best_main_c - 1e-14:
                best_main_c = cu
                best_main_h = h_up

            rem = opt_deadline - time.time()
            if rem > 90.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
                desired = 0.08 * work_total
                short_time = min(desired, rem - 80.0)
                if short_time <= 25.0:
                    short_time = max(25.0, rem * 0.25)
                if short_time > 20.0:
                    h3, c3 = minimax_projected(best_main_h, n_main, N_m, valid_m, F_m,
                                               time.time() + short_time, int(seed) + 31,
                                               stop_c=target_met)
                    if float(c3) < best_main_c - 1e-14:
                        best_main_c = float(c3)
                        best_main_h = h3

    rem = opt_deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 150.0 and best_main_c > target_met:
        n_final = 4096 if n_main >= 2048 else 2048
        N_fin, valid_fin, F_fin = precompute_fft(n_final)
        h_fin = resample_h(best_main_h, n_final)

        desired = 0.30 * work_total
        fin_time = min(desired, rem - 70.0)
        if fin_time <= 50.0:
            fin_time = max(50.0, rem * 0.5)

        if fin_time > 40.0:
            end3 = time.time() + fin_time
            if end3 > opt_deadline:
                end3 = opt_deadline
            h4, c4 = minimax_projected(h_fin, n_final, N_fin, valid_fin, F_fin,
                                       end3, int(seed) + 41, stop_c=target_met)
            high_h = h4
            high_n = n_final

    best_h = project_h(best_main_h, n_main)
    best_exact = float(compute_c5_exact(best_h, n_main))
    best_n = n_main

    if have_init:
        if c_init_exact < best_exact:
            best_h = project_h(h_init, n_init)
            best_exact = c_init_exact
            best_n = n_init

    if high_h is not None and time.time() < hard_deadline - 10.0:
        high_h = project_h(high_h, high_n)
        ce = float(compute_c5_exact(high_h, high_n))
        if ce < best_exact:
            best_h = high_h
            best_exact = ce
            best_n = high_n

    return best_h, float(best_exact), int(best_n)
```
